# Wardogs — Gameplay Tag Definitions (v1)
*Extracted from the game's packed config files ([`Wardogs/Config/`](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/tree/main/data/Wardogs/Config)). Source copies are in `data/Wardogs/Config/`. Each entry is a `GameplayTagList` line.*

> **Scope:** the tag names and dev comments defined in [`DefaultGameplayTags.ini`](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/blob/main/data/Wardogs/Config/DefaultGameplayTags.ini) and [`Config/Tags/`](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/tree/main/data/Wardogs/Config/Tags). Engine tags are not included. How tags match at runtime is engine logic and is not in these files.

| `Debug.Director.PlacedFX` | Set on gameplay cues placed by the Spectrum Director filming tool, so a cue can tell a hand-placed debug spawn from a real gameplay hit. The bullet-impact cue uses it to take its immediate-spawn FX path instead of the batched Niagara Data Channel path, whose live-fire perception and de-duplication gates silently drop a single hand-placed impact. | `DefaultGameplayTags.ini` |

| `Achievement.Combat.DefibrillatorKill` | Unlocks on a kill with the defibrillator. | `Tags/Meta/MetaChallengeGameplayTags.ini` |
| `Achievement.Combat.LongShot` | Unlocks on a kill from at least the row's distance. | `Tags/Meta/MetaChallengeGameplayTags.ini` |
| `Achievement.Combat.RicochetKill` | Unlocks on a kill from a ricocheted projectile. | `Tags/Meta/MetaChallengeGameplayTags.ini` |
| `Achievement.Economy.LoadoutSpend` | Unlocks when a single loadout purchase reaches the row's threshold. | `Tags/Meta/MetaChallengeGameplayTags.ini` |
| `Achievement.Economy.SingleLifeProfit` | Unlocks when the player banks the row's net profit threshold within one life. | `Tags/Meta/MetaChallengeGameplayTags.ini` |
| `Achievement.Economy.TopCashEarner` | Unlocks when the player finishes a match as the top cash earner. | `Tags/Meta/MetaChallengeGameplayTags.ini` |
| `Achievement.Match.CleanSweep` | Unlocks when the player's faction wins the match without the opposition scoring. | `Tags/Meta/MetaChallengeGameplayTags.ini` |
| `Achievement.Survival.ControlZoneSurvivor` | Unlocks after surviving the row's duration inside the control zone without dying. | `Tags/Meta/MetaChallengeGameplayTags.ini` |
| `Achievement.Tutorial.Completed` | Unlocks when the player completes the tutorial. | `Tags/Meta/MetaChallengeGameplayTags.ini` |
| `Achievement.Tutorial.PunchKristopher` | Unlocks when the player punches Kristopher during the tutorial. | `Tags/Meta/MetaChallengeGameplayTags.ini` |

---

## 3. How the values combine

- Tags are dotted names. The dots are the only hierarchy stored in these files: `Meta.Damage.AmmoModifier.HollowPoint` sits under `Meta.Damage.AmmoModifier`. Matching rules are not in the files.
- Other docs in this set use these tags as keys (for example the `Tag` and `RewardTag` columns in the reward lookups, and the damage requirements in the vehicle damage tables).

---

## 4. Still not pulled / limitations

- **Dev comments:** most tags have an empty `DevComment`. The comment column is blank for those.
- **Matching rules:** how a requirement tag matches its child tags, and how tags are applied at runtime, are engine logic and are not in these files.
- **Redirects:** `GameplayTagRedirects` entries are not listed here.
